HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_104d25f613df9597dec7","text":"2. **Servanthood as honored, willing belonging.** **Evidence/cue:** `ع ب د B003` is worship and submissive obedience, while `ع ب د B006` is honoring and magnifying; 89:28 has already established mutual approval (`ر ض و B001/B003`), and 89:26 supplied coercive restraint. **Mechanism:** service gives the group its shared orientation, but acceptance and honor distinguish that orientation from forced bondage. The packet supplies obedience, approval, and the coercive contrast; I infer willing belonging. **Reading change:** *ʿibadi* names a community dignified by shared service, not an undifferentiated owned mass.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000464/B003","root_000464/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_34657ea8a9403efaea83","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:29:1\",\"root_000464\",[\"B003\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Giriş, yalnız dışarıdan bir topluluğa katılmayı değil, ruhun gizli niyetinin de bu aidiyete kabul edilmesini içerir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000464/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_48bb9d1eb7a598332781","text":"3. **The inner self entering an inner relation.** **Evidence/cue:** `د خ ل B003` is the hidden interior of an affair, and the entrant is the *nafs*, whose `ن ف س B013` branch names inward thought and intent. **Mechanism:** the interior branch assigns entry a transformation of inward allegiance as well as social placement. The packet supplies inner self and entry; I infer that genuine incorporation reaches intention. **Reading change:** the command addresses belonging from the inside, not outward enrollment alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000464/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5c8a28955d7b6606d6a0","text":"1. **Entry as incorporation into a people.** **Evidence/cue:** `د خ ل B001` is going in, while `د خ ل B005` is an entrant mixed into a group or its affairs; the construction uses *fi* with the plural *ʿibadi*, not a place-name. **Mechanism:** entry's endpoint is membership, with the former singular self incorporated into a plural body. The packet supplies singular command and collective complement; I infer social integration as the result. **Reading change:** the first *udkhuli* means “become one among,” not merely cross a threshold.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000464/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_754d87da555a00a1ef1d","text":"89:30 clarified why the first complement is a people rather than a place: social incorporation precedes environmental entry. The second *udkhuli* therefore confirms `د خ ل B005` in 89:29 as group-membership, not an elliptical reference to the garden.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a5ee61d71b91f8730735","text":"89:30 sharpened this ayah’s sequence: entry into servants is the social threshold before entry into garden; belonging precedes sheltered abundance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000464/B005","root_000973/B003","root_000973/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_dc9fc7e1bde0a8b280b0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:29:1\",\"root_000464\",[\"B005\"]],[\"89:29:3\",\"root_000973\",[\"B003\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kulların arasına giriş, tekil ruhu ortak yönelişle hizmet eden ve bu hizmetle onur kazanan bir topluluğun üyesi yapar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000464/B001","root_000464/B005","root_000973/B003","root_000973/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_eccd629a09dd4bcb494f","text":"1. **Entry into a service-body, not isolation.** Lexical evidence: `د خ ل B001` going in/entering; `د خ ل B005` entrant mixed into a group; `ع ب د B003` worship and submissive obedience; `ع ب د B006` honoring and magnifying. Structural cue: the command follows the singular soul but moves it into `عبادي`, a collective belonging. Assigned role: entry repairs the earlier orphaned/odd isolation by joining the soul to a community of service. Abductive move: I infer that the group is defined by worshipful belonging rather than social domination, because the speaker is the Lord of return. Reading change: the ayah reads as honored incorporation, not loss of self.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000464/B003","root_000464/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f3fe1956943bfa02ea23","text":"2. **Hidden interior purified of defect.** Lexical evidence: `د خ ل B003` hidden interior; `د خ ل B004` hidden defect or deceit; `ن ف س B013` inner mind/intent. Structural cue: the tranquil self is commanded to enter after its inner valuation has been resolved. Assigned role: entry exposes and admits the interior that no longer carries the corrupt hidden defect. Abductive move: I infer that final inclusion requires inward truthfulness, because earlier speech concealed false doctrine. Reading change: `ادخلي` becomes an inward admission as well as spatial entry.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
